%include "qb_table.qbi"
:i function $BurnBurn_PryamidHip01Script$
    :i doIf $Triggertype$ :a{ $TRIGGER_JUMP_OFF$ $TRIGGER_SKATE_OFF_EDGE$ :a}
        :i $StartGap$ $GapID$ = $JKU_Burn_GAP_ID_BridgeGrind01$ $flags$ = :a{ $CANCEL_GROUND$ $CANCEL_MANUAL$ $CANCEL_WALL$ :a}
    :i endif

:i endfunction

:i function $BurnBurn_QP07Script$
    :i $StartGap$ $GapID$ = $JKU_Burn_Gap_LipTrick02$ $flags$ = :a{ $CANCEL_GROUND$ $CANCEL_WALL$ $CANCEL_MANUAL$ :a}
    :i $StartGap$ $GapID$ = $JKU_Burn_Gap_LipTrick03$ $flags$ = :a{ $CANCEL_GROUND$ $CANCEL_WALL$ $CANCEL_MANUAL$ :a}

:i endfunction

:i function $BurnSkBurn_Twinkie02Script$
    :i doIf $Triggertype$ :a{ $TRIGGER_JUMP_OFF$ $TRIGGER_SKATE_OFF_EDGE$ :a}
        :i $StartGap$ $GapID$ = $JKU_Burn_GAP_ID_Twinkie01$ $flags$ = :a{ $CANCEL_GROUND$ $CANCEL_MANUAL$ $CANCEL_WALL$ :a}
    :i endif

:i endfunction

:i function $BurnTRG_Burn_Ped01Script$
    :i $Ped_SetDefaults$
    :i $SetTags$ $collision_mode$ = $Avoid$
    :i $SetTags$ $is_moving_ped$ = %i(0,00000000)
    :i $SetTags$ $Idle$ = $THPS3_Peds_ScratchChin$
    :i $SetTags$ $IdleAnims$ = :a{$THPS3_Peds_ScratchChin$$THPS3_Peds_LookAtWatch$$THPS3_Peds_LookLeftRight$$THPS3_Peds_LookRight$$THPS3_Peds_LookLeftDown$$THPS3_Peds_Idle$$THPS3_Peds_Idle2$$THPS3_Peds_Idle3$:a}
    :i $SetTags$ $RotateFromIdle1$ = $THPS3_Peds_ScratchChin$
    :i $SetTags$ $RotateFromIdle2$ = $THPS3_Peds_ScratchChin$
    :i $SetTags$ $RotateFromIdle3$ = $THPS3_Peds_ScratchChin$
    :i $SetTags$ $DisgustAnims$ = :a{$THPS3_Peds_ScratchChin$:a}
    :i $ped_initialize_collision_exceptions$
    :i $Obj_StickToGround$ $distAbove$ = %i(3,00000003) $distBelow$ = %i(6,00000006)
    :i $Obj_StickToGround$ $off$
    :i $ped_initialize_movement$
    :i $goto$ $Ped_IdleState$

:i endfunction

:i function $BurnTRG_Burn_Rail100Script$
    :i doIf $Triggertype$ :a{ $TRIGGER_LIP_ON$ :a}
        :i $EndGap$ $GapID$ = $JKU_Burn_Gap_LipTrick02$ $text$ = %s(9,"Main Wall") $score$ = %i(500,000001f4)
    :i endif

:i endfunction
